# Vulkan structures by command

Generated by `scripts/study_command_structs.py`.

Registry: `external/Vulkan-Headers/registry/vk.xml` (API `vulkan`).
Registry SHA-256: `a04d755984966b0cbae852ccb13874ab03f6779206beac941739caf91fa46a85`.

842 command names, 737 canonical commands, 1401 distinct canonical structures/unions.

Scope: all core versions and API-supported extensions in this registry, including platform and provisional extensions; independent of the cvk policy.

Filters: all command names.

`direct` means a typed command parameter; `member` means a recursively contained structure/union; `pNext` identifies an allowed extender and its chain base. Paths use `.` for member access, `[]` for arrays, and `pNext<Type>` for a node anywhere in that base's chain (not a required order). Nested members and their own chains are included. No Info/Query name filter is applied, so feature/property outputs and plain structures are retained.

Parameter declarations retain constness; non-const pointers may be output or input/output. Alias commands share a section; structure aliases are listed below.

This is a registry relationship inventory, not a list of combinations legal on a particular device. Extension/feature enablement, platform guards, union selection, ignored members, and command-specific valid usage still apply. Opaque void payloads, callback signatures, and relationships described only in specification prose are not inferred.

The [Khronos registry schema](https://registry.khronos.org/vulkan/specs/latest/registry.html) defines `structextends` against the base of the whole chain; extender relations are therefore not followed transitively.
